## [8.0.0](https://github.com/alrayyes/forge-dashboard/compare/v7.0.0...v8.0.0) (2026-10-27)


### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES

* `conclusion` is no longer limited to `success`, `failure`, `cancelled`, `skipped` and `timed_out`. It can also be `neutral`, `stale`, `startup_failure` or `action_required`, and a finished `action_required` job is `status: completed` rather than `waiting`. A client that switches on the five words needs to handle the rest.

### Features

* send the conclusion the forge reported ([#2983](https://github.com/alrayyes/forge-dashboard/issues/2983)) ([659f327](https://github.com/alrayyes/forge-dashboard/commit/659f327b0c410b72b0a2e5214c3c399e8733aec8))

## [7.0.0](https://github.com/alrayyes/forge-dashboard/compare/v6.1.0...v7.0.0) (2026-10-27)


### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES

* the stream's named `checks` event is gone. A client reads `run` events instead. `RunEvent` replaces `CheckListEvent` in the OpenAPI spec.

### Features

* stream a run event per run attempt ([#2981](https://github.com/alrayyes/forge-dashboard/issues/2981)) ([bb1995f](https://github.com/alrayyes/forge-dashboard/commit/bb1995f135f084d773de2ec4497f1871afc14285))

## [6.1.0](https://github.com/alrayyes/forge-dashboard/compare/v6.0.0...v6.1.0) (2026-10-27)


### Features

* send GitHub run attempt and Forgejo job times ([#2979](https://github.com/alrayyes/forge-dashboard/issues/2979)) ([ac5dc9e](https://github.com/alrayyes/forge-dashboard/commit/ac5dc9e8a336bf39e4a9171e839ba87b471065fd))

## [6.0.0](https://github.com/alrayyes/forge-dashboard/compare/v5.0.0...v6.0.0) (2026-10-27)


### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES

* a stream or MCP check no longer has `state`. It has `status` plus `conclusion`: `queued` stays `queued`, `running` becomes `in_progress`, `awaiting_approval` becomes `waiting`, and `success`, `failure`, `cancelled`, `skipped` and `timed_out` become `completed` with that conclusion. Clients to update: forge-dashboard-sdk-go, forge-dashboard-sdk-node, forge-dashboard-sdk-php, forge-dashboard-sdk-python and forge-board-cloud-api.

### Features

* send check status and conclusion ([#2976](https://github.com/alrayyes/forge-dashboard/issues/2976)) ([6fe8578](https://github.com/alrayyes/forge-dashboard/commit/6fe857812bd6e52c747999c09cd08c841ccfe160))

## [5.0.0](https://github.com/alrayyes/forge-dashboard/compare/v4.0.1...v5.0.0) (2026-10-27)


### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES

* a client that took a `runId` on a check to mean "can still be cancelled" must read the check's `state` instead. Affects forge-dashboard-sdk-go, forge-dashboard-sdk-node, forge-dashboard-sdk-php, forge-dashboard-sdk-python and forge-board-cloud-api.

### Features

* name the run on every job ([#2972](https://github.com/alrayyes/forge-dashboard/issues/2972)) ([7e1051e](https://github.com/alrayyes/forge-dashboard/commit/7e1051e9412a8c8198f440c12763aa82e16f513b))

## [4.0.0](https://github.com/alrayyes/forge-dashboard/compare/v3.1.0...v4.0.0) (2026-10-27)


### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES

* `components.schemas.Check` loses `durationSeconds` and gains `startedAt` and `completedAt` (date-time). This reaches forge-dashboard-sdk-go, forge-dashboard-sdk-node, forge-dashboard-sdk-php, forge-dashboard-sdk-python and forge-board-cloud-api.
